Use when Yevster says 'studio request: …', 'ask the studio for …', 'prioritize X', 'move #N up', 'studio feedback: …', 'that run was too slow / wasteful / …', 'studio verdict: …', 'keep / kill the power-ups', or 'what have I asked the studio for'.
